       27: function drawTs(draw) {
       28:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
       29:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));

>>>    32:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
       33:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
       34:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
       35:   const add = mins >= 0 ? mins * 60 * 1000 : 0;
       36:   return base + add;
       37: }

       39: function drawKey(draw) {
       40:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
       41:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
       42:   if (!y || !h) return "";
       43:   return `${y}|${h}`;
       44: }

       46: function dedupeAndSort(draws) {
       47:   const map = new Map();
       48: 
       49:   for (const d of Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []) {
       50:     const key = drawKey(d);
       51:     if (!key) continue;
       52:     if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, d);
       53:   }
       54: 
       55:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
       56: }
       57: 
       58: function top5Groups(draw) {
       59:   const out = [];
       60: 
       61:   for (const p of Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : []) {
       62:     const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
       63:     const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(p));
       64: 
       65:     if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 5) continue;
       66:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) continue;
       67: 
       68:     out.push({ pos, grupo: g });
       69:   }
       70: 
       71:   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
       72: }
       73: 
       74: function ensureRow(map, key) {

       76:     map.set(key, { total: 0, hitTop5: 0, hitFirst: 0 });
       77:   }
       78: 
       79:   return map.get(key);
       80: }

      186:   let totalCases = 0;
      187:   let hitTop5 = 0;
      188:   let hitFirst = 0;
      189: 
      190:   const byTargetHour = new Map();
      191: 
      192:   for (let i = 0; i < allDraws.length - 1; i += 1) {
      193:     const drawLast = allDraws[i];
      194:     const history = allDraws.slice(0, i + 1);
      195: 
      196:     const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
      197:     const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
      198: 
      199:     if (!lastY || !lastH) continue;
      200: 
>>>   201:     const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
>>>   202:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
      203:       ymd: lastY,
      204:       hourBucket: lastH,
>>>   205:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
>>>   206:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
>>>   207:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
      208:     });
      209: 
      210:     if (!nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
      211: 
      212:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
>>>   213:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
      214:       startSlot: { ymd: nextSlot.ymd, hour: nextSlot.hour },
      215:       drawsIndex,
>>>   216:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
>>>   217:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
>>>   218:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
      219:     });
      220: 
      221:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
      222:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
      223: 
      224:     if (drawKey(history[history.length - 1]) === drawKey(nextDraw)) {
      225:       throw new Error(`Vazamento de futuro no histórico: ${drawKey(nextDraw)}`);
      226:     }
      227: 
>>>   228:     const computed = computeConditionalNextTop3({
>>>   229:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
      230:       drawsRange: history,
      231:       drawLast,
>>>   232:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
>>>   233:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
>>>   234:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
      235:       topN: 3,
      236:     });
      237: 
      238:     assertComputedSlotMatchesNextDraw({
      239:       computed,
      240:       found,
      241:       nextSlot,
      242:       drawLast,
      243:     });
      244: 
      245:     assertPredictionIntegrity({
      246:       computed,
      247:       drawLast,
      248:       nextDraw,
      249:     });
      250: 
      251:     const pred = Array.isArray(computed?.top)
>>>   252:       ? computed.top.slice(0, 3).map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
      253:       : [];
      254: 
      255:     const actualTop5 = top5Groups(nextDraw);
>>>   256:     const actualGroups = actualTop5.map((x) => Number(x.grupo));
      257:     const actualFirst = actualTop5.find((x) => x.pos === 1)?.grupo ?? null;
      258: 
      259:     if (!actualGroups.length || !Number.isFinite(Number(actualFirst))) {
      260:       continue;
      261:     }
      262: 
      263:     const hasTop5 = pred.some((g) => actualGroups.includes(g));
      264:     const hasFirst = pred.includes(Number(actualFirst));
      265: 
      266:     totalCases += 1;
      267:     if (hasTop5) hitTop5 += 1;
      268:     if (hasFirst) hitFirst += 1;
      269: 
      270:     const targetHour =
      271:       found?.hour || found?.slot?.hour || pickDrawHour(nextDraw) || "";
      272: 
      273:     const row = ensureHourRow(byTargetHour, targetHour);
      274:     row.total += 1;
      275:     if (hasTop5) row.hitTop5 += 1;
      276:     if (hasFirst) row.hitFirst += 1;
      277:   }
      278: 
>>>   279:   console.log("\n================ BACKTEST TOP3 PT_RIO ================");
      280:   console.log(`Janela: ${from} até ${maxDate}`);
      281:   console.log(`Casos testados: ${totalCases}`);
      282:   console.log(
>>>   283:     `Acerto TOP3 dentro do TOP5: ${hitTop5} / ${totalCases} = ${pct(
      284:       hitTop5,
      285:       totalCases
      286:     )}%`
      287:   );
      288:   console.log(
>>>   289:     `Acerto TOP3 contendo o 1º prêmio: ${hitFirst} / ${totalCases} = ${pct(
      290:       hitFirst,
      291:       totalCases
      292:     )}%`
      293:   );
      294:   console.log("------------------------------------------------------");
      295: 
      296:   for (const [hour, row] of Array.from(byTargetHour.entries()).sort()) {
      297:     console.log(
      298:       `${hour} | casos=${row.total} | top5=${row.hitTop5}/${row.total} (${pct(
      299:         row.hitTop5,
      300:         row.total
      301:       )}%) | primeiro=${row.hitFirst}/${row.total} (${pct(
      302:         row.hitFirst,
      303:         row.total
      304:       )}%)`
      305:     );
      306:   }

       23: function normalizeSchedule(hours) {
       24:   return [...hours]
>>>    25:     .map((hour) => {
       26:       const value = String(hour).trim();
       27: 
       28:       if (/^\d{1,2}h$/.test(value)) {
       29:         return (
       30:           value
       31:             .replace("h", "")
       32:             .padStart(2, "0")
       33:           + ":00"
       34:         );
       35:       }
       36: 
       37:       if (/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(value)) {
       38:         const [hh, mm] = value.split(":");
       39: 
       40:         return `${hh.padStart(2, "0")}:${mm}`;
       41:       }
       42: 
       43:       return value;
       44:     })
       45:     .sort();
       46: }

      125: function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {

      144:     (probability * 0.55)

      162:     ],

      165:       ...strength.signals,

      167:     },

>>>    51: function sanitizeTop3(top) {
       52:   const out = [];
       53:   const seen = new Set();
       54: 
       55:   for (const item of Array.isArray(top) ? top : []) {
       56:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
       57: 
       58:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) continue;
       59: 
       60:     const grupo = Math.trunc(g);
       61:     if (seen.has(grupo)) continue;
       62: 
       63:     const confidence = firstFiniteNumber(
       64:       item?.displayConfidence,
       65:       item?.confidence,
       66:       item?.scoreProb,
       67:       item?.prob,
       68:       0
       69:     );
       70: 
       71:     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence) || confidence < 0) continue;
       72: 
       73:     seen.add(grupo);
       74: 
       75:     out.push({
       76:       ...item,
       77:       grupo,
       78:       displayConfidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.displayConfidence))
       79:         ? Number(item.displayConfidence)
       80:         : confidence,
       81:       confidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.confidence))
       82:         ? Number(item.confidence)
       83:         : confidence,
       84:       scoreProb: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.scoreProb))
       85:         ? Number(item.scoreProb)
       86:         : confidence,
       87:     });
       88: 
       89:     if (out.length >= 3) break;
       90:   }
       91: 
       92:   return out;
       93: }

        1: function clampProb(v) {
        2:   const n = Number(v);
        3: 
        4:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
        5:   if (n > 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / 100));
        6: 
        7:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
        8: }
        9: 
       10: function isValidGrupo(g) {
       11:   const n = Number(g);
       12:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25;
       13: }
       14: 
>>>    15: export function buildTop3Predictions({
       16:   analytics,
       17:   build20,
       18:   safeStr,
       19:   toHourBucket,
       20:   getAnimalLabel,

       22:   resolveProbValue,

       24:   buildResultStyleImgVariants,

       26:   const arr = Array.isArray(analytics?.top) ? analytics.top : [];
       27:   const milharesCache = new Map();
       28: 
       29:   return arr
       30:     .filter((x) => isValidGrupo(x?.grupo))
       31:     .slice(0, 3)
>>>    32:     .map((x) => {
       33:       const g = Number(x.grupo);
       34:       const animal = safeStr(getAnimalLabel(g) || "");
       35: 
       36:       const nextY = safeStr(x?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
       37:       const nextH = toHourBucket
       38:         ? toHourBucket(x?.meta?.next?.hour || "")
       39:         : safeStr(x?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
       40: 
       41:       const cacheKey = `${g}|${nextY}|${nextH}`;
       42: 
       43:       let out = milharesCache.get(cacheKey);
       44:

       46:         out = build20(g, x);
       47:         milharesCache.set(cacheKey, out);
       48:       }
       49:

       51:       const milhares20 = milharesCols.flatMap((c) => c.items).slice(0, 20);
       52:

       18: function clampProb(v) {
       19:   const n = Number(v);
       20:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
       21:   if (n > 1) return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / 100));
       22:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
       23: }
       24: 
       25: function resolveProbValue(x) {
       26:   return clampProb(x?.scoreProb ?? x?.prob ?? x?.probCond ?? 0);

       31:   if (!dig) return "";
       32:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
       33: }

        1: import {
        2:   pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw,
        3:   pickDrawYMD,
        4:   pickDrawHour,
>>>     5: } from "./top3.engine";
        6: 
>>>     7: import { isYMD, toHourBucket, hourToInt } from "./top3.formatters";
        8: 
        9: /*
>>>    10: TOP3+ — Cadeia de Bichos
       11: Modelo de transição de grupos
       12: 
       13: - Entende targetYmd / targetHourBucket
       14: - Encontra o draw imediatamente anterior ao alvo
       15: - Separa transições por faixa de horário
       16: - Evita ordenar sorteio sem hora como 00h
       17: - Evita vazamento usando apenas histórico anterior ao alvo no modo target_aware
       18: */
       19: 
       20: function safeInt(v, fallback = 0) {
       21:   const n = Number(v);
       22:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
       23: }
       24: 
       25: function drawSortTs(draw) {
       26:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
       27:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
       28: 
       29:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
       30: 
       31:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
       32:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
       33: 
>>>    34:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
       35:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
       36: 
       37:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
       38: }
       39: 
       40: function slotTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
       41:   const safeYmd = String(ymd || "").trim();
       42:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
       43: 
       44:   if (!isYMD(safeYmd) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
       45: 
       46:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
       47:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
       48: 
>>>    49:   const [Y, M, D] = safeYmd.split("-").map(Number);
       50:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
       51: 
       52:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
       53: }
       54: 
       55: function dedupeAndSortDraws(draws) {
       56:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
       57:   const map = new Map();
       58: 
       59:   for (const d of list) {
       60:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
       61:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
       62:     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
       63: 
       64:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
       65: 
       66:     const key =
       67:       String(d?.id || d?.drawId || "").trim() ||
       68:       (isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "");
       69: 
       70:     if (!key) continue;
       71: 
       72:     const prev = map.get(key);
       73: 
       74:     if (!prev || ts < drawSortTs(prev)) {
       75:       map.set(key, d);
       76:     }
       77:   }
       78: 
       79:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawSortTs(a) - drawSortTs(b));

       84:   const to = toHourBucket(toHour);
       85: 
       86:   if (!from || !to) return "";
       87: 
       88:   return `${from}->${to}`;
       89: }

       91: function getNestedMap(map, key) {
       92:   if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, new Map());
       93:   return map.get(key);

      159:   }

      164: function rankTransitionMap(rowMap, basedOnGrupo, topN) {
      165:   if (!rowMap || !rowMap.size) return [];
      166: 
      167:   const total = Array.from(rowMap.values()).reduce(
      168:     (acc, n) => acc + safeInt(n, 0),
      169:     0
      170:   );
      171: 
      172:   if (total <= 0) return [];
      173: 
      174:   return Array.from(rowMap.entries())
      175:     .sort((a, b) => {
      176:       if (Number(b[1]) !== Number(a[1])) return Number(b[1]) - Number(a[1]);
      177:       return Number(a[0]) - Number(b[0]);
      178:     })
      179:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, safeInt(topN, 3)))
>>>   180:     .map(([grupo, freq], i) => {
      181:       const prob = Number(freq) / total;
      182: 
      183:       return {
      184:         rank: i + 1,
      185:         grupo: Number(grupo),
      186:         freq: Number(freq),
      187:         prob,
      188:         probPct: Number((prob * 100).toFixed(2)),
      189:         basedOnGrupo: Number(basedOnGrupo),
      190:         totalTransitionsFromGrupo: total,
      191:       };
      192:     });
      193: }

      195: export function predictNextGrupoFromChain(arg1, arg2 = 3) {
      196:   if (Array.isArray(arg1)) {
      197:     const draws = arg1;
      198:     const topN = arg2;
      199: 
      200:     const list = dedupeAndSortDraws(draws);
      201:     if (!list.length) return [];
      202: 
      203:     const { transitionsAny } = computeChainTransitions(list);
      204:     const lastDraw = list[list.length - 1];
      205:     const lastGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(lastDraw));
      206: 
      207:     if (!Number.isFinite(lastGrupo) || lastGrupo < 1 || lastGrupo > 25) {
      208:       return [];
      209:     }
      210: 
      211:     const rowMap = transitionsAny.get(lastGrupo);
      212:     const ranked = rankTransitionMap(rowMap, lastGrupo, topN);
      213: 
>>>   214:     return ranked.map((x) => ({
      215:       ...x,
      216:       mode: "legacy_any",
      217:       basedOnHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(lastDraw)),
      218:       basedOnYmd: pickDrawYMD(lastDraw),
      219:       targetHour: "",
      220:       targetYmd: "",
      221:       transitionType: "ANY",
      222:       transitionEdge: "",
      223:     }));
      224:   }
      225: 
      226:   const { draws, targetYmd, targetHourBucket, topN = 3 } = arg1 || {};
      227: 
      228:   const targetHour = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
      229:   const safeTargetYmd = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
      230: 
      231:   if (!isYMD(safeTargetYmd) || !targetHour) return [];
      232: 
      233:   const historyBeforeTarget = getHistoryBeforeTarget(
      234:     draws,
      235:     safeTargetYmd,
      236:     targetHour
      237:   );
      238: 
      239:   if (!historyBeforeTarget.length) return [];
      240: 
      241:   const prevDraw = historyBeforeTarget[historyBeforeTarget.length - 1];
      242:   if (!prevDraw) return [];
      243: 
      244:   const prevGrupo = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(prevDraw));
      245:   const prevHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prevDraw));
      246:   const prevYmd = pickDrawYMD(prevDraw);
      247: 
      248:   if (
      249:     !Number.isFinite(prevGrupo) ||
      250:     prevGrupo < 1 ||
      251:     prevGrupo > 25 ||
      252:     !prevHour ||
      253:     !isYMD(prevYmd)
      254:   ) {
      255:     return [];
      256:   }
      257: 
      258:   const { transitionsAny, transitionsByEdge } =
      259:     computeChainTransitions(historyBeforeTarget);
      260: 
      261:   const edgeKey = makeEdgeKey(prevHour, targetHour);
      262:   const edgeMap = edgeKey ? transitionsByEdge.get(edgeKey) || null : null;
      263:   const edgeRow = edgeMap ? edgeMap.get(prevGrupo) : null;
      264: 
      265:   let ranked = rankTransitionMap(edgeRow, prevGrupo, topN);
      266:   let transitionType = "EDGE";
      267: 
      268:   if (!ranked.length) {
      269:     const anyRow = transitionsAny.get(prevGrupo) || null;
      270:     ranked = rankTransitionMap(anyRow, prevGrupo, topN);
      271:     transitionType = "ANY_FALLBACK";
      272:   }
      273: 
>>>   274:   return ranked.map((x) => ({
      275:     ...x,
      276:     mode: "target_aware",
      277:     basedOnHour: prevHour,
      278:     basedOnYmd: prevYmd,
      279:     targetHour,
      280:     targetYmd: safeTargetYmd,
      281:     transitionType,
      282:     transitionEdge: edgeKey,
      283:   }));
      284: }

       56: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
       57:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
       58:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
       59: 
       60:   const n = Number(s);
       61:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
       62:   if (n === 0) return 25;
       63:

       65:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
       66: }

       68: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
       69:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
       70:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
       71:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
       72: 
       73:   let score = 0;
       74:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
       75:   if (h) score += 20;
       76:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
       77: 
       78:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
       79:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
       80: 
       81:   return score;
       82: }

      548: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
      549:   const map = new Map();
      550:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
      551: 
      552:   for (const d of list) {
      553:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
      554:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
      555:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
      556: 
      557:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
      558: 
      559:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
      560:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
      561: 
      562:     if (!prev) {
      563:       map.set(drawKey, d);
      564:       continue;
      565:     }
      566: 
      567:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
      568:       map.set(drawKey, d);
      569:     }
      570:   }
      571: 
      572:   return map;
      573: }

      649: function buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw) {
      650:   const freq = new Map();
      651:   const firstMap = new Map();
      652:   const items = getAllTop5Groups(nextDraw);
      653:

      658:     }

      664: function mergeMapsAdd(target, source) {
      665:   for (const [k, v] of source.entries()) {
      666:     target.set(Number(k), Number(target.get(Number(k)) || 0) + Number(v || 0));
      667:   }
      668: }

>>>   670: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      671:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      672:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
      673:   const out = new Map();
      674: 
      675:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
      676:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
      677:     out.set(g, n / denom);
      678:   }
      679: 
      680:   return out;
      681: }

      684:   draws,

      737: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
      738:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
      739:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      740:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
      741:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
      742:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
      743:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
      744:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
      745:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
      746:     return tsa - tsb;
      747:   });
      748: }
      749: 
      750: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
      751:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
      752:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
      753:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
      754:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
      755: 
      756:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
      757:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
      758:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
      759:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
      760:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
      761:   });
      762: 
      763:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
      764: }

>>>   766: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      767:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      768:   const out = new Map();
      769: 
      770:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
      771:     out.set(g, {
      772:       recentTop5: 0,
      773:       recentIndirect: 0,
      774:       recentFirst: 0,
      775:       recentLast1First: 0,
      776:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
      777:     });
      778:   }
      779: 
      780:   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
      781:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
      782:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
      783: 
      784:   for (const d of list) {
      785:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
      786: 
      787:     for (const it of items) {
      788:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      789:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
      790:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      791: 
      792:       const row = out.get(g);
      793:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
      794: 
      795:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
      796:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
      797:     }
      798:   }
      799: 
      800:   for (const d of last1) {
      801:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
      802:     for (const it of items) {
      803:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      804:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
      805:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      806:       if (pos === 1) {
      807:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
      808:       }
      809:     }
      810:   }
      811: 
      812:   for (const d of last2) {
      813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
      814:     const seen = new Set();
      815:     for (const it of items) {
      816:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      817:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      818:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
      819:       seen.add(g);
      820:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
      821:     }
      822:   }
      823: 
      824:   return out;
      825: }

      827: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
      828:   const v = Number(value || 0);
      829:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
      830:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
      831:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
      832: }
      833: 
      834: function clamp01(v) {
      835:   const n = Number(v);
      836:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
      837:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
      838: }

>>>   840: function buildZeroProbMap(groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      841:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      842:   return new Map(Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => [i + 1, 0]));
      843: }

      845: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
      846:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
      847:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
      848:   return clamp01(n / lim);
      849: }

     1445: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
     1446:   draws,
>>>  1447:   lotteryKey,
     1448:   targetHour,
     1449:   targetDow,
>>>  1450:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
     1451: ) {
     1452:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
>>>  1453:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
     1454:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
     1455:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     1456: 
     1457:   const firstFreq = new Map();
     1458:   let totalSamples = 0;
     1459: 
     1460:   for (const d of list) {
     1461:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     1462:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     1463:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
     1464: 
>>>  1465:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
>>>  1466:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
     1467:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
     1468:     } else {
     1469:       if (h !== target) continue;
     1470:     }
     1471: 
     1472:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
     1473:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
     1474: 
     1475:     totalSamples += 1;
     1476:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
     1477:   }
     1478: 
     1479:   const prob = new Map();
     1480:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
     1481: 
     1482:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     1483:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
     1484:   }
     1485: 
     1486:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
     1487: }

     1546: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {

     1558: function getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 6) {
     1559:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];

     1564: 
     1565: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
     1566:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
     1567:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
     1568:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
     1569: 
     1570:   let matched = 0;
     1571:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
     1572:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
     1573:     matched += 1;
     1574:   }
     1575:   return matched;
     1576: }

     1578: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
     1579:   drawsRange,
>>>  1580:   lotteryKey,
     1581:   targetHour,
     1582:   targetDow,
     1583:   currentState,
>>>  1584:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
     1585: }) {
     1586:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
     1587:     drawsRange,
>>>  1588:     lotteryKey,
     1589:     targetHour,
     1590:     targetDow
     1591:   );
     1592: 
     1593:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     1594:   const freq = new Map();
     1595: 
     1596:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     1597:     freq.set(g, 0);
     1598:   }
     1599: 
     1600:   let totalWeight = 0;
     1601:   let matchedSamples = 0;
     1602: 
     1603:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
     1604:     const target = seq[i];
     1605:     const histState = [];
     1606: 
     1607:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
     1608:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
     1609:     }
     1610: 
     1611:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
     1612:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
     1613: 
     1614:     const weight =
     1615:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
     1616:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
     1617:       1.0;
     1618: 
     1619:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
     1620:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
     1621: 
     1622:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
     1623:     totalWeight += weight;
     1624:     matchedSamples += 1;

     1644: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
     1645:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
     1646:   const groups = list
>>>  1647:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
     1648:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
     1649: 
     1650:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
     1651:     return {
     1652:       regime: "neutral",
     1653:       repeatRate: 0,
     1654:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
     1655:       samples: groups.length,
     1656:     };
     1657:   }
     1658: 
     1659:   let repeats = 0;
     1660:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
     1661:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
     1662:   }
     1663: 
     1664:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
     1665:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
     1666: 
     1667:   let regime = "neutral";
     1668: 
     1669:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
     1670:     regime = "repeat";
     1671:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
     1672:     regime = "spread";
     1673:   }
     1674: 
     1675:   return {
     1676:     regime,
     1677:     repeatRate,
     1678:     uniqueRate,
     1679:     samples: groups.length,
     1680:   };
     1681: }

     2842:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
     2843: }
     2844: 
     2845: function emptyGroupMap() {
     2846:   const out = new Map();
>>>  2847:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
     2848:   return out;
     2849: }
     2850: 
     2851: function getTop5GroupSet(draw) {
     2852:   return new Set(
     2853:     getAllTop5Groups(draw)
>>>  2854:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
>>>  2855:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
     2856:   );
     2857: }
     2858: 
>>>  2859: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
     2860:   const out = new Map();
>>>  2861:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
     2862:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
     2863:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
     2864:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
     2865: 
     2866:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     2867:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
     2868:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
     2869:   }
     2870: 
     2871:   return out;
     2872: }
     2873: 
     2874: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
     2875:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
     2876:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
     2877:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
     2878: }

     3243: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
     3244:   const candidates = [
     3245:     prize?.milhar,
     3246:     prize?.milhar4,
     3247:     prize?.numero,
     3248:     prize?.number,
     3249:     prize?.value,
     3250:     prize?.result,
     3251:     prize?.resultado,
     3252:     prize?.premio,
     3253:   ];
     3254: 
     3255:   for (const v of candidates) {
     3256:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
     3257:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
     3258:   }
     3259: 
     3260:   return "";
     3261: }
     3262: 
     3263: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
     3264:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
     3265:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
     3266: 
     3267:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
     3268:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
     3269: 
     3270:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
     3271:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
     3272: 
     3273:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
     3274: }
     3275:

     3327: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
     3328:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
     3329: 
     3330:   let score = 0;
     3331:   const details = {};
     3332: 
     3333:   const add = (key, value) => {
     3334:     if (!value) return;
     3335:     score += value;
     3336:     details[key] = value;
     3337:   };
     3338: 
     3339:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
     3340:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
     3341: 
     3342:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
     3343:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
     3344: 
     3345:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
     3346:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
     3347: 
     3348:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
     3349:     add("firstGrupo",45);
     3350: 
     3351:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
     3352:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
     3353:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
     3354:   }
     3355: 
     3356:   add(
     3357:     "groupOverlap",
     3358:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
     3359:   );
     3360: 
     3361:   add(
     3362:     "dezenaOverlap",
     3363:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
     3364:   );
     3365: 
     3366:   add(
     3367:     "centenaOverlap",
     3368:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
     3369:   );
     3370: 
     3371:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
     3372:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
     3373: 
     3374:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
     3375:     add("repeatExists",8);
     3376: 
     3377:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
     3378:     add("repeatEqual",6);
     3379: 
     3380:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
     3381:     add("signature",60);
     3382: 
     3383:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
     3384:     add("hour",8);
     3385: 
     3386:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
     3387: }

>>>  3437: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
     3438:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
     3439:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     3440: 
     3441:   const freq = new Map();
     3442:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
     3443: 
     3444:   let totalWeight = 0;
     3445: 
     3446:   for (const item of ranking) {
     3447:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
     3448:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
     3449: 
     3450:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
     3451:     if (score <= 0) continue;
     3452: 
     3453:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
     3454:     totalWeight += score;
     3455:   }
     3456: 
     3457:   const prob = new Map();
     3458:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     3459:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
     3460:   }
     3461: 
     3462:   return {
     3463:     freq,
     3464:     prob,
     3465:     samples: ranking.length,
     3466:     totalWeight,
     3467:   };
     3468: }

       32: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
       33:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
       34:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
       35:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
       36: 
       37:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
       38: 
       39:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
       40: 
       41:   return list
       42:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
>>>    43:     .map((draw) => {

       56: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
       57:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
       58:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
       59: 
       60:   const n = Number(s);
       61:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
       62:   if (n === 0) return 25;
       63: 
       64:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
       65:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
       66: }
       67: 
       68: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
       69:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
       70:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
       71:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
       72: 
       73:   let score = 0;
       74:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
       75:   if (h) score += 20;
       76:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
       77: 
       78:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
       79:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
       80: 
       81:   return score;
       82: }
       83: 
       84: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
       85:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
       86:     ? Number(p.position)
       87:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
       88:       ? Number(p.posicao)
       89:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
       90:         ? Number(p.pos)
       91:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
       92:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
       93:           : null;
       94: 
       95:   return pos;
       96: }

      195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
      196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
      197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
      198: 
      199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      200: 
      201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
      202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
      203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      204:   }
      205: 
>>>   206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
      207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
      208: 
      209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
      210: }

      548: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
      549:   const map = new Map();
      550:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
      551: 
      552:   for (const d of list) {
      553:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
      554:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
      555:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
      556: 
      557:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
      558: 
      559:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
      560:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
      561: 
      562:     if (!prev) {
      563:       map.set(drawKey, d);
      564:       continue;
      565:     }
      566: 
      567:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
      568:       map.set(drawKey, d);
      569:     }
      570:   }
      571: 
      572:   return map;
      573: }

      649: function buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw) {
      650:   const freq = new Map();

      653:

      664: function mergeMapsAdd(target, source) {
      665:   for (const [k, v] of source.entries()) {
      666:     target.set(Number(k), Number(target.get(Number(k)) || 0) + Number(v || 0));
      667:   }

>>>   670: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      671:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      672:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
      673:   const out = new Map();
      674: 
      675:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
      676:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
      677:     out.set(g, n / denom);
      678:   }
      679: 
      680:   return out;
      681: }

      737: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
      738:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
      739:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      740:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
      741:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
      742:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
      743:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
      744:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
      745:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
      746:     return tsa - tsb;
      747:   });
      748: }

      750: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
      751:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
      752:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
      753:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
      754:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
      755: 
      756:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
      757:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
      758:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
      759:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
      760:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
      761:   });
      762: 
      763:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
      764: }

>>>   766: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      767:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      768:   const out = new Map();
      769: 
      770:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
      771:     out.set(g, {
      772:       recentTop5: 0,
      773:       recentIndirect: 0,
      774:       recentFirst: 0,
      775:       recentLast1First: 0,
      776:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
      777:     });
      778:   }
      779: 
      780:   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
      781:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
      782:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
      783: 
      784:   for (const d of list) {
      785:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
      786: 
      787:     for (const it of items) {
      788:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      789:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
      790:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      791: 
      792:       const row = out.get(g);
      793:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
      794: 
      795:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
      796:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
      797:     }
      798:   }
      799: 
      800:   for (const d of last1) {
      801:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
      802:     for (const it of items) {
      803:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      804:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
      805:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      806:       if (pos === 1) {
      807:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
      808:       }
      809:     }
      810:   }
      811: 
      812:   for (const d of last2) {
      813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
      814:     const seen = new Set();
      815:     for (const it of items) {
      816:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      817:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      818:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
      819:       seen.add(g);
      820:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
      821:     }
      822:   }
      823: 
      824:   return out;
      825: }

      827: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
      828:   const v = Number(value || 0);
      829:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
      830:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
      831:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
      832: }
      833: 
      834: function clamp01(v) {
      835:   const n = Number(v);
      836:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
      837:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
      838: }

>>>   840: function buildZeroProbMap(groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      841:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      842:   return new Map(Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => [i + 1, 0]));
      843: }

      845: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
      846:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
      847:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
      848:   return clamp01(n / lim);
      849: }

     1445: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
     1446:   draws,
>>>  1447:   lotteryKey,
     1448:   targetHour,
     1449:   targetDow,

>>>  1453:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
     1454:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
     1455:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     1456: 
     1457:   const firstFreq = new Map();
     1458:   let totalSamples = 0;
     1459: 
     1460:   for (const d of list) {
     1461:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     1462:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     1463:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
     1464: 
>>>  1465:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
>>>  1466:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
     1467:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
     1468:     } else {
     1469:       if (h !== target) continue;
     1470:     }
     1471: 
     1472:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
     1473:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
     1474: 
     1475:     totalSamples += 1;
     1476:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
     1477:   }
     1478: 
     1479:   const prob = new Map();
     1480:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
     1481: 
     1482:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     1483:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
     1484:   }
     1485: 
     1486:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
     1487: }

     1564: 
     1565: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
     1566:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
     1567:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
     1568:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
     1569: 
     1570:   let matched = 0;
     1571:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
     1572:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
     1573:     matched += 1;
     1574:   }
     1575:   return matched;
     1576: }

     1578: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
     1579:   drawsRange,
>>>  1580:   lotteryKey,
     1581:   targetHour,
     1582:   targetDow,
     1583:   currentState,
>>>  1584:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
     1585: }) {
     1586:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
     1587:     drawsRange,
>>>  1588:     lotteryKey,
     1589:     targetHour,
     1590:     targetDow
     1591:   );
     1592: 
     1593:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     1594:   const freq = new Map();
     1595: 
     1596:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     1597:     freq.set(g, 0);
     1598:   }
     1599: 
     1600:   let totalWeight = 0;
     1601:   let matchedSamples = 0;
     1602: 
     1603:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
     1604:     const target = seq[i];
     1605:     const histState = [];
     1606: 
     1607:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
     1608:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
     1609:     }
     1610: 
     1611:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
     1612:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
     1613: 
     1614:     const weight =
     1615:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
     1616:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
     1617:       1.0;
     1618: 
     1619:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
     1620:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
     1621: 
     1622:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
     1623:     totalWeight += weight;
     1624:     matchedSamples += 1;
     1625:   }
     1626: 
     1627:   const prob = new Map();
     1628:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalWeight);
     1629: 
     1630:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     1631:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
     1632:   }
     1633: 
     1634:   return {
     1635:     prob,
     1636:     freq,
     1637:     totalWeight,
     1638:     matchedSamples,
     1639:     state: Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [],
     1640:     sequenceSamples: seq.length,
     1641:   };
     1642: }

     1644: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
     1645:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
     1646:   const groups = list
>>>  1647:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
     1648:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
     1649: 
     1650:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
     1651:     return {
     1652:       regime: "neutral",
     1653:       repeatRate: 0,
     1654:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
     1655:       samples: groups.length,
     1656:     };
     1657:   }
     1658: 
     1659:   let repeats = 0;
     1660:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
     1661:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
     1662:   }
     1663: 
     1664:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
     1665:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
     1666: 
     1667:   let regime = "neutral";
     1668: 
     1669:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
     1670:     regime = "repeat";
     1671:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
     1672:     regime = "spread";
     1673:   }
     1674: 
     1675:   return {
     1676:     regime,
     1677:     repeatRate,
     1678:     uniqueRate,
     1679:     samples: groups.length,
     1680:   };
     1681: }

     2856:   );

     2858: 
>>>  2859: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
     2860:   const out = new Map();
>>>  2861:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
     2862:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
     2863:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
     2864:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
     2865: 
     2866:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     2867:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
     2868:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
     2869:   }
     2870: 
     2871:   return out;
     2872: }
     2873: 
     2874: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
     2875:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
     2876:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
     2877:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
     2878: }
     2879:

     3243: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
     3244:   const candidates = [
     3245:     prize?.milhar,
     3246:     prize?.milhar4,
     3247:     prize?.numero,
     3248:     prize?.number,
     3249:     prize?.value,
     3250:     prize?.result,
     3251:     prize?.resultado,
     3252:     prize?.premio,
     3253:   ];
     3254: 
     3255:   for (const v of candidates) {
     3256:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
     3257:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
     3258:   }
     3259: 
     3260:   return "";
     3261: }
     3262: 
     3263: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
     3264:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
     3265:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
     3266: 
     3267:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
     3268:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
     3269: 
     3270:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
     3271:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
     3272: 
     3273:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
     3274: }
     3275: 
     3276: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
     3277:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
     3278: 
     3279:   const rows = prizes

     3304:     return Array.from(m.entries())

     3326: 
     3327: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
     3328:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
     3329: 
     3330:   let score = 0;
     3331:   const details = {};
     3332: 
     3333:   const add = (key, value) => {
     3334:     if (!value) return;
     3335:     score += value;
     3336:     details[key] = value;
     3337:   };
     3338: 
     3339:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
     3340:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
     3341: 
     3342:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
     3343:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
     3344: 
     3345:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
     3346:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
     3347: 
     3348:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
     3349:     add("firstGrupo",45);
     3350: 
     3351:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
     3352:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
     3353:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
     3354:   }
     3355: 
     3356:   add(
     3357:     "groupOverlap",
     3358:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
     3359:   );
     3360: 
     3361:   add(
     3362:     "dezenaOverlap",
     3363:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
     3364:   );
     3365: 
     3366:   add(
     3367:     "centenaOverlap",
     3368:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
     3369:   );
     3370: 
     3371:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
     3372:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
     3373: 
     3374:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
     3375:     add("repeatExists",8);
     3376: 
     3377:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
     3378:     add("repeatEqual",6);
     3379: 
     3380:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
     3381:     add("signature",60);
     3382: 
     3383:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
     3384:     add("hour",8);
     3385:

>>>  3437: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
     3438:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
     3439:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     3440: 
     3441:   const freq = new Map();
     3442:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
     3443: 
     3444:   let totalWeight = 0;
     3445: 
     3446:   for (const item of ranking) {
     3447:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
     3448:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
     3449: 
     3450:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
     3451:     if (score <= 0) continue;
     3452: 
     3453:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
     3454:     totalWeight += score;
     3455:   }
     3456: 
     3457:   const prob = new Map();
     3458:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     3459:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
     3460:   }
     3461: 
     3462:   return {
     3463:     freq,
     3464:     prob,
     3465:     samples: ranking.length,
     3466:     totalWeight,
     3467:   };
     3468: }

     4025:         return tb - ta;
     4026:       })[0] || null;
     4027: 
     4028:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
     4029: 
     4030:     if (!baseDraw) {
     4031:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
     4032:         .filter((d) => {
     4033:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     4034:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     4035:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
     4036:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
     4037:         })
     4038:         .sort((a, b) => {
     4039:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
     4040:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
     4041:           return tb - ta;
     4042:         })[0] || null;
     4043: 
     4044:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
     4045:     }
     4046: 
     4047:     if (!baseDraw) {
     4048:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
     4049:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
     4050:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     4051:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     4052:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
     4053: 
     4054:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
     4055: 
     4056:         baseDraw = d;
     4057:         break;
     4058:       }
     4059:     }
     4060: 
     4061:     if (!baseDraw) {
     4062:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
     4063:     }
     4064: 
     4065:     if (!baseDraw) {
     4066:       timeline.push({
     4067:         targetYmd,
     4068:         targetHour: slotHour,
     4069:         baseYmd: "",
     4070:         baseHour: "",
>>>  4071:         top3: [],
     4072:         resultGrupo: null,
     4073:         hit: null,
     4074:         status: "pending",
     4075:       });
     4076:       continue;
     4077:     }
     4078: 
     4079:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
     4080:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
     4081:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
     4082:     );
     4083: 
     4084:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
     4085:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
     4086:         pickDrawYMD(d),
     4087:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
     4088:       );
     4089:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
     4090:     });
     4091: 
     4092:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
     4093:       .filter((d) => {
     4094:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
     4095:           pickDrawYMD(d),
     4096:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
     4097:         );
     4098:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
     4099:       })
     4100:       .sort((a, b) => {

     4102:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));

       32: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
       33:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
       34:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
       35:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
       36: 
       37:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
       38: 
       39:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
       40: 
       41:   return list
       42:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
>>>    43:     .map((draw) => {
       44:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
       45:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
       46:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
       47:     })
       48:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
       49:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
       50: }

       56: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
       57:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
       58:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
       59: 
       60:   const n = Number(s);
       61:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
       62:   if (n === 0) return 25;
       63: 
       64:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
       65:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
       66: }
       67: 
       68: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
       69:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
       70:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
       71:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
       72: 
       73:   let score = 0;
       74:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
       75:   if (h) score += 20;
       76:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
       77: 
       78:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
       79:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
       80: 
       81:   return score;
       82: }
       83: 
       84: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
       85:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
       86:     ? Number(p.position)
       87:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
       88:       ? Number(p.posicao)
       89:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
       90:         ? Number(p.pos)
       91:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
       92:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
       93:           : null;
       94: 
       95:   return pos;
       96: }

      195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
      196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
      197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
      198: 
      199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      200: 
      201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
      202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
      203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      204:   }
      205: 
>>>   206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
      207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
      208: 
      209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
      210: }

      664: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
      665:   const map = new Map();
      666:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
      667: 
      668:   for (const d of list) {
      669:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
      670:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
      671:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
      672: 
      673:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
      674: 
      675:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
      676:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
      677: 
      678:     if (!prev) {
      679:       map.set(drawKey, d);
      680:       continue;
      681:     }
      682: 
      683:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
      684:       map.set(drawKey, d);
      685:     }
      686:   }
      687: 
      688:   return map;
      689: }

>>>   786: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      787:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      788:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
      789:   const out = new Map();
      790: 
      791:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
      792:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
      793:     out.set(g, n / denom);
      794:   }
      795: 
      796:   return out;
      797: }

      853: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
      854:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
      855:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      856:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
      857:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
      858:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
      859:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
      860:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
      861:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
      862:     return tsa - tsb;
      863:   });
      864: }

      866: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
      867:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
      868:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
      869:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
      870:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
      871: 
      872:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
      873:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
      874:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
      875:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
      876:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
      877:   });
      878: 
      879:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
      880: }

>>>   882: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
      883:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
      884:   const out = new Map();
      885: 
      886:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
      887:     out.set(g, {
      888:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
      889:       recentIndirect: 0,
      890:       recentFirst: 0,
      891:       recentLast1First: 0,
      892:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
      893:     });
      894:   }
      895:

      897:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
      898:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
      899: 
      900:   for (const d of list) {
      901:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
      902: 
      903:     for (const it of items) {
      904:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      905:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
      906:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      907:

      909:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
      910: 
      911:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
      912:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
      913:     }
      914:   }
      915: 
      916:   for (const d of last1) {
      917:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
      918:     for (const it of items) {
      919:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      920:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
      921:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      922:       if (pos === 1) {
      923:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
      924:       }
      925:     }
      926:   }
      927: 
      928:   for (const d of last2) {
      929:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
      930:     const seen = new Set();
      931:     for (const it of items) {
      932:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
      933:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
      934:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
      935:       seen.add(g);
      936:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
      937:     }
      938:   }
      939: 
      940:   return out;
      941: }

      943: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
      944:   const v = Number(value || 0);
      945:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
      946:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
      947:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
      948: }
      949: 
      950: function clamp01(v) {
      951:   const n = Number(v);
      952:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
      953:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
      954: }

      961: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
      962:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
      963:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
      964:   return clamp01(n / lim);

     1560: 
     1561: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
     1562:   draws,
>>>  1563:   lotteryKey,
     1564:   targetHour,
     1565:   targetDow,
>>>  1566:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
     1567: ) {
     1568:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
>>>  1569:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
     1570:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
     1571:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     1572: 
     1573:   const firstFreq = new Map();
     1574:   let totalSamples = 0;
     1575: 
     1576:   for (const d of list) {
     1577:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     1578:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     1579:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
     1580: 
>>>  1581:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
>>>  1582:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
     1583:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
     1584:     } else {
     1585:       if (h !== target) continue;
     1586:     }
     1587: 
     1588:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
     1589:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
     1590: 
     1591:     totalSamples += 1;
     1592:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
     1593:   }
     1594: 
     1595:   const prob = new Map();
     1596:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
     1597: 
     1598:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     1599:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
     1600:   }
     1601: 
     1602:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
     1603: }

     1605: function getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
     1606:   draws,
>>>  1607:   lotteryKey,
     1608:   targetHour,
     1609:   targetDow

     1611:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);

     1613:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
     1614: 
     1615:   return list.filter((d) => {
     1616:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     1617:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     1618:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
     1619: 
>>>  1620:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
>>>  1621:       return isFederalDrawDay(y) && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
     1622:     }
     1623: 
     1624:     return h === target;
     1625:   });
     1626: }

     1662: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {
     1663:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
     1664:   const n = Math.max(1, Number(count || 3));

     1681: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
     1682:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
     1683:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
     1684:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
     1685: 
     1686:   let matched = 0;

     1688:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;

     1690:   }
     1691:   return matched;

     1760: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
     1761:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
     1762:   const groups = list
>>>  1763:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
     1764:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
     1765: 
     1766:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
     1767:     return {
     1768:       regime: "neutral",
     1769:       repeatRate: 0,
     1770:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
     1771:       samples: groups.length,
     1772:     };
     1773:   }
     1774: 
     1775:   let repeats = 0;
     1776:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
     1777:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
     1778:   }
     1779: 
     1780:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
     1781:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
     1782: 
     1783:   let regime = "neutral";
     1784: 
     1785:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
     1786:     regime = "repeat";
     1787:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
     1788:     regime = "spread";
     1789:   }
     1790: 
     1791:   return {
     1792:     regime,
     1793:     repeatRate,
     1794:     uniqueRate,
     1795:     samples: groups.length,
     1796:   };
     1797: }

     2894:           key: layer.key,

     2899:       },
     2900:     },

     2904: 
     2905: /* =========================
     2906:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
     2907: ========================= */
     2908: 
     2909: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
     2910:   const k = Number(key);
>>>  2911:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
     2912:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
     2913: }
     2914: 
     2915: function emptyGroupMap() {
     2916:   const out = new Map();
>>>  2917:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
     2918:   return out;
     2919: }
     2920: 
     2921: function getPrizePresenceGroupSet(draw) {
     2922:   return new Set(
     2923:     getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw)
>>>  2924:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
>>>  2925:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
     2926:   );
     2927: }
     2928: 
>>>  2929: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
     2930:   const out = new Map();
>>>  2931:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
     2932:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
     2933:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
     2934:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
     2935: 
     2936:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     2937:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
     2938:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
     2939:   }
     2940: 
     2941:   return out;
     2942: }
     2943: 
     2944: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
     2945:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
     2946:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
     2947:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
     2948: }

     3309: /* =========================

     3312: 
     3313: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
     3314:   const candidates = [
     3315:     prize?.milhar,
     3316:     prize?.milhar4,
     3317:     prize?.numero,
     3318:     prize?.number,
     3319:     prize?.value,
     3320:     prize?.result,
     3321:     prize?.resultado,
     3322:     prize?.premio,
     3323:   ];
     3324: 
     3325:   for (const v of candidates) {
     3326:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
     3327:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
     3328:   }
     3329: 
     3330:   return "";
     3331: }
     3332: 
     3333: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
     3334:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
     3335:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
     3336: 
     3337:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
     3338:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
     3339: 
     3340:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
     3341:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
     3342: 
     3343:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
     3344: }
     3345: 
     3346: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
     3347:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
     3348: 
     3349:   const rows = prizes
>>>  3350:     .map((p) => {
     3351:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
     3352:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
     3353:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
     3354: 
     3355:       return {
     3356:         pos,
     3357:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
     3358:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
     3359:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
     3360:         grupo,
     3361:       };
     3362:     })
     3363:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
     3364:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
     3365: 
>>>  3366:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
>>>  3367:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
>>>  3368:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
>>>  3369:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
     3370: 
     3371:   const countMap = (arr) => {
     3372:     const m = new Map();
     3373:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
     3374:     return Array.from(m.entries())
     3375:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
>>>  3376:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
     3377:   };

     3379:   return {
     3380:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
     3381:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
     3382:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
     3383:     grupos,
     3384:     dezenas,
     3385:     centenas,
     3386:     milhares,
     3387:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
     3388:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
     3389:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
     3390:     rows,
>>>  3391:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
     3392:   };
     3393: }

     3396: 
     3397: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
     3398:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
     3399: 
     3400:   let score = 0;
     3401:   const details = {};
     3402: 
     3403:   const add = (key, value) => {
     3404:     if (!value) return;
     3405:     score += value;
     3406:     details[key] = value;
     3407:   };
     3408: 
     3409:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
     3410:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
     3411: 
     3412:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
     3413:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
     3414: 
     3415:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
     3416:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
     3417: 
     3418:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
     3419:     add("firstGrupo",45);
     3420: 
     3421:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
     3422:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
     3423:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
     3424:   }
     3425: 
     3426:   add(
     3427:     "groupOverlap",
     3428:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
     3429:   );
     3430: 
     3431:   add(
     3432:     "dezenaOverlap",
     3433:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
     3434:   );
     3435: 
     3436:   add(
     3437:     "centenaOverlap",
     3438:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
     3439:   );
     3440: 
     3441:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
     3442:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
     3443: 
     3444:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
     3445:     add("repeatExists",8);
     3446: 
     3447:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
     3448:     add("repeatEqual",6);
     3449: 
     3450:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
     3451:     add("signature",60);
     3452: 
     3453:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
     3454:     add("hour",8);
     3455: 
     3456:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
     3457: }
