>>>  3507: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
     3508:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
     3509:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
     3510: 
     3511:   const freq = new Map();
     3512:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
     3513: 
     3514:   let totalWeight = 0;
     3515: 
     3516:   for (const item of ranking) {
     3517:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
     3518:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
     3519: 
     3520:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
     3521:     if (score <= 0) continue;
     3522: 
     3523:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
     3524:     totalWeight += score;
     3525:   }
     3526: 
     3527:   const prob = new Map();
     3528:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
     3529:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
     3530:   }
     3531: 
     3532:   return {
     3533:     freq,
     3534:     prob,
     3535:     samples: ranking.length,
     3536:     totalWeight,
     3537:   };
     3538: }

     3550:   const out = [];

     3553:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
     3554:   }
     3555: 
     3556:   return out.filter(Boolean);
     3557: }

     4064:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
     4065:       .filter((d) => {
     4066:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     4067:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     4068:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
     4069:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
     4070:       })
     4071:       .sort((a, b) => {
     4072:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
     4073:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
     4074:         return tb - ta;
     4075:       })[0] || null;
     4076: 
     4077:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
     4078: 
     4079:     if (!baseDraw) {
     4080:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
     4081:         .filter((d) => {
     4082:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     4083:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));

     4085:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
     4086:         })
     4087:         .sort((a, b) => {
     4088:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
     4089:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
     4090:           return tb - ta;
     4091:         })[0] || null;
     4092: 
     4093:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
     4094:     }
     4095:

     4097:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
     4098:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
     4099:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
     4100:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
     4101:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
     4102: 
     4103:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
     4104: 
     4105:         baseDraw = d;
     4106:         break;
     4107:       }
     4108:     }
     4109: 
     4110:     if (!baseDraw) {
     4111:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
     4112:     }
     4113: 
     4114:     if (!baseDraw) {
     4115:       timeline.push({
     4116:         targetYmd,
     4117:         targetHour: slotHour,
     4118:         baseYmd: "",
     4119:         baseHour: "",
>>>  4120:         top3: [],
     4121:         resultGrupo: null,
     4122:         hit: null,
     4123:         status: "pending",
     4124:       });
     4125:       continue;
     4126:     }
     4127: 
     4128:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
     4129:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
     4130:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
     4131:     );
     4132: 
     4133:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
     4134:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
     4135:         pickDrawYMD(d),
     4136:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
     4137:       );
     4138:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
     4139:     });
     4140: 
     4141:     const historyStats = (() => {
     4142:       const MS_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
     4143: 
     4144:       let d30 = 0;
     4145:       let d90 = 0;
     4146:       let d180 = 0;
     4147:       let d365 = 0;
     4148: 
     4149:       for (const historicalDraw of usableHistory) {
     4150:         const historicalTs = ymdHourToTs(
     4151:           pickDrawYMD(historicalDraw),
     4152:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(historicalDraw))
     4153:         );
     4154: 
     4155:         if (!Number.isFinite(historicalTs)) continue;
     4156: 
     4157:         const diffDays = (baseTs - historicalTs) / MS_DAY;
     4158: 
     4159:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30) d30 += 1;
     4160:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 90) d90 += 1;
     4161:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 180) d180 += 1;
     4162:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 365) d365 += 1;
     4163:       }
     4164:

     4168:         d90,

     4178:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))

       52: function cleanFirestoreValue(value) {
       53:   try {
       54:     return JSON.parse(
       55:       JSON.stringify(value, (_key, current) => {
       56:         if (current === undefined) return null;
       57:         if (typeof current === "number" && !Number.isFinite(current)) {
       58:           return null;
       59:         }
       60:         return current;
       61:       })
       62:     );
       63:   } catch {
       64:     return null;
       65:   }

       15: export function isYMD(s) {
       16:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
       17:   const m = str.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
       18:   if (!m) return false;
       19: 
       20:   const y = Number(m[1]);
       21:   const mo = Number(m[2]);
       22:   const d = Number(m[3]);
       23: 
       24:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
       25:   return (
       26:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
       27:     dt.getUTCMonth() === mo - 1 &&
       28:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
       29:   );
       30: }

       38: }

      147: function isValidHourMinute(hh, mm) {
      148:   const h = Number(hh);
      149:   const m = Number(mm);
      150: 
      151:   return (
      152:     Number.isFinite(h) &&
      153:     Number.isFinite(m) &&
      154:     h >= 0 &&
      155:     h <= 23 &&
      156:     m >= 0 &&
      157:     m <= 59
      158:   );
      159: }

      161: /**
      162:  * Normaliza diversos formatos para "HH:MM".
      163:  */
      164: export function normalizeHourLike(value) {
      165:   const s0 = safeStr(value);
      166:   if (!s0) return "";
      167: 
      168:   const s = s0
      169:     .replace(/\s+/g, "")
      170:     .replace(/[–—]/g, "-")
      171:     .toLowerCase();
      172: 
      173:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
      174:   if (mhx) {
      175:     const hh = Number(mhx[1]);
      176:     return isValidHourMinute(hh, 0) ? `${pad2(hh)}:00` : "";
      177:   }
      178: 
      179:   const mhm = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)(\d{1,2})$/i);
      180:   if (mhm) {
      181:     const hh = Number(mhm[1]);
      182:     const mm = Number(mhm[2]);
      183:     return isValidHourMinute(hh, mm) ? `${pad2(hh)}:${pad2(mm)}` : "";
      184:   }
      185: 
      186:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
      187:   if (mISO) {
      188:     const hh = Number(mISO[1]);
      189:     const mm = Number(mISO[2]);
      190:     return isValidHourMinute(hh, mm) ? `${pad2(hh)}:${pad2(mm)}` : "";

       10:

       18: export function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
       19:   const s0 = safeStr(src);
       20:   if (!s0) return "";
       21: 
       22:   if (/^(https?:)?\/\//i.test(s0)) return s0;
       23:   if (/^(data:|blob:)/i.test(s0)) return s0;
       24: 
       25:   const base = publicBase();
       26: 
       27:   let s = s0;
       28: 
       29:   s = s.replace(/^(\.\/)+/, "");
       30:   s = s.replace(/^(\.\.\/)+/, "");
       31: 
       32:   if (base) {
       33:     if (s === base) return s;
       34:     if (s.startsWith(base + "/")) return s;
       35:   }
       36: 
       37:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return joinPublic(base, s);
       38:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return joinPublic(base, s.slice("public/".length));
       39: 
       40:   return joinPublic(base, s);
       41: }

       24: function makeKey(ymd, hour) {

       30: function parseTargetDate(ymd, hour) {
       31:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return null;
       32:

       34:   const m = String(hourBucket || "").match(/^(\d{2})h$/);
       35:   if (!m) return null;

>>>    37:   const [Y, M, D] = String(ymd).split("-").map(Number);
       38:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
       39:

       41:   return Number.isNaN(dt.getTime()) ? null : dt;
       42: }

       44: function isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) {
       45:   const target = parseTargetDate(ymd, hour);
       46:   if (!target) return false;
       47:   return target.getTime() > Date.now();
       48: }

       50: function resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks) {
       51:   const hasPicks = Array.isArray(picks) && picks.length > 0;
       52:   if (!hasPicks) return "empty";
       53:   return isFutureTarget(ymd, hour) ? "future" : "pending_result";
       54: }

       67:   const n = Number(resultValue);

       71: function normalizeLogEntry(entry) {
       72:   if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") return null;
       73: 
       74:   const ymd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
       75:   const hour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");
       76:   const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);
       77: 
       78:   if (!targetKey) return null;
       79: 
       80:   const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);
       81:   const result = normalizeResult(entry?.result);
       82: 
       83:   const hit =
       84:     result != null && picks.length
       85:       ? picks.includes(result)
       86:       : typeof entry?.hit === "boolean"
       87:         ? entry.hit
       88:         : null;
       89: 
       90:   const status =
       91:     result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);
       92: 
       93:   return {
       94:     ...entry,
       95:     targetKey,
       96:     target: { ymd, hour },
       97:     picks,
       98:     result,
       99:     hit,
      100:     createdAt: Number(entry?.createdAt) || Date.now(),
      101:     validatedAt:
      102:       result != null && Number(entry?.validatedAt)
      103:         ? Number(entry.validatedAt)
      104:         : undefined,
      105:     status,
      106:   };
      107: }
      108: 
      109: function scoreLogEntry(entry) {
      110:   if (!entry) return -1;
      111: 
      112:   let score = 0;
      113:   if (Array.isArray(entry?.picks) && entry.picks.length) score += 20;
      114:   if (entry?.result != null) score += 10;

      119:   return score;
      120: }
      121: 
      122: function normalizeAndDedupeLog(log) {
      123:   const list = Array.isArray(log) ? log : [];
      124:   const byKey = new Map();
      125: 
      126:   for (const raw of list) {
      127:     const entry = normalizeLogEntry(raw);
      128:     if (!entry) continue;
      129: 
      130:     const prev = byKey.get(entry.targetKey);
      131: 
      132:     if (!prev || scoreLogEntry(entry) >= scoreLogEntry(prev)) {
      133:       byKey.set(entry.targetKey, entry);
      134:     }
      135:   }
      136: 
      137:   return [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => {
      138:     const aKey = `${a?.target?.ymd || ""}_${a?.target?.hour || ""}`;
      139:     const bKey = `${b?.target?.ymd || ""}_${b?.target?.hour || ""}`;
      140:     return aKey < bKey ? -1 : aKey > bKey ? 1 : 0;
      141:   });
      142: }
      143:

      147:     return normalizeAndDedupeLog(raw);
      148:   } catch {
      149:     return [];
      150:   }
      151: }

      157:   } catch {}

      160: function registerPrediction({
      161:   targetKey,
      162:   targetYmd,
      163:   targetHour,
      164:   picks,
      165:   snapshot = [],
      166:   engineVersion = "",
      167: }) {
      168:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
      169:   const hour = normHour(targetHour);

      171: 
      172:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour || !normalizedKey) return;

      174:   const normalizedPicks = normalizePicks(picks);
      175:   if (!normalizedPicks.length) return;

>>>   177:   const log = getTop3Log();
      178: 
      179:   const idx = log.findIndex(
      180:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedKey)
      181:   );

      184: 
      185:   if (idx >= 0) {

      188:     if (prev.result != null || prev.status === "validated") return;

      191:       ...prev,
      192:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
      193:       target: { ymd, hour },

      196:       hit: null,

      201:             ? snapshot
      202:             : [],

      204:         safeStr(prev?.engineVersion || engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
      205:       createdAt: prev.createdAt || Date.now(),
      206:       status,
      207:     };
      208:   } else {
      209:     log.push({
      210:       targetKey: normalizedKey,
      211:       target: { ymd, hour },
      212:       picks: normalizedPicks,
      213:       result: null,
      214:       hit: null,
      215:       snapshot: Array.isArray(snapshot) ? snapshot : [],
      216:       engineVersion: safeStr(engineVersion || "V3_STATISTICAL"),
      217:       createdAt: Date.now(),
      218:       status,
      219:     });
      220:   }
      221: 
>>>   222:   saveTop3Log(log);
      223: }

      225: function registerResult({ targetKey, resultGrupo }) {
>>>   226:   const log = getTop3Log();
      227:   const normalizedTargetKey = safeStr(targetKey);
      228:   const result = normalizeResult(resultGrupo);
      229: 
      230:   if (!normalizedTargetKey || result == null) return;
      231: 
      232:   const idx = log.findIndex(
      233:     (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(normalizedTargetKey)
      234:   );
      235: 
      236:   if (idx === -1) return;

      238:   const picks = normalizePicks(log[idx]?.picks);
      239: 
      240:   log[idx] = {
      241:     ...log[idx],
      242:     picks,
      243:     result,
      244:     hit: picks.includes(result),
      245:     validatedAt: Date.now(),
      246:     status: "validated",
      247:   };
      248: 
>>>   249:   saveTop3Log(log);
      250: }

      252: function findRealDrawByTarget({ targetYmd, targetHour, todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
      253:   const ymd = safeStr(targetYmd);
      254:   const hour = normHour(targetHour);
      255: 
      256:   if (!isYMD(ymd) || !hour) return null;
      257: 
      258:   const sameTarget = (d) =>
      259:     pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd && normHour(pickDrawHour(d)) === hour;
      260: 
      261:   const realToday =
      262:     (Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;
      263: 
      264:   const realRange =
      265:     (Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : []).find(sameTarget) || null;
      266: 
      267:   return realToday || realRange || null;
      268: }
      269: 
>>>   270: function reconcilePendingTop3Log({ todayDraws, rangeDraws }) {
>>>   271:   const log = getTop3Log();
      272:   if (!Array.isArray(log) || !log.length) return;
      273: 
      274:   let changed = false;
      275: 
>>>   276:   const nextLog = log.map((entry) => {
      277:     if (!entry || entry.result != null) return entry;
      278: 
      279:     const targetYmd = safeStr(entry?.target?.ymd || "");
      280:     const targetHour = normHour(entry?.target?.hour || "");
      281: 
      282:     if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !targetHour) return entry;
      283:     if (isFutureTarget(targetYmd, targetHour)) return entry;
      284: 
      285:     const real = findRealDrawByTarget({
      286:       targetYmd,
      287:       targetHour,
      288:       todayDraws,
      289:       rangeDraws,
      290:     });
      291: 
      292:     if (!real) return entry;
      293: 
      294:     const result = normalizeResult(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(real));
      295:     if (result == null) return entry;
      296: 
      297:     const picks = normalizePicks(entry?.picks);
      298: 
      299:     changed = true;
      300: 
      301:     return {
      302:       ...entry,
      303:       picks,
      304:       result,
      305:       hit: picks.includes(result),
      306:       validatedAt: Date.now(),
      307:       status: "validated",
      308:     };
      309:   });
      310: 
>>>   311:   if (changed) saveTop3Log(nextLog);
      312: }
      313: 
>>>   314: function ensureDayTimeline({ ymd, lotteryKey }) {
      315:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return;
      316: 
>>>   317:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
>>>   318:     lotteryKey,
      319:     ymd,
>>>   320:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
>>>   321:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
>>>   322:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
      323:   });
      324: 
      325:   if (!Array.isArray(schedule) || !schedule.length) return;
      326: 
>>>   327:   const log = getTop3Log();
      328: 
      329:   for (const h of schedule) {
      330:     const hour = normHour(h);
      331:     const targetKey = makeKey(ymd, hour);
      332: 
      333:     if (!targetKey) continue;
      334: 
      335:     const idx = log.findIndex(
      336:       (l) => String(l?.targetKey || "") === String(targetKey)
      337:     );
      338: 
      339:     if (idx >= 0) {
      340:       const prev = log[idx] || {};
      341:       const picks = normalizePicks(prev?.picks);
      342:       const result = normalizeResult(prev?.result);
      343:       const status =
      344:         result != null ? "validated" : resolvePendingStatus(ymd, hour, picks);
      345: 
      346:       log[idx] = {
      347:         ...prev,
      348:         targetKey,
      349:         target: { ymd, hour },
      350:         picks,
      351:         result,
      352:         hit: result != null ? picks.includes(result) : prev?.hit ?? null,
      353:         createdAt: prev?.createdAt || Date.now(),
      354:         validatedAt: result != null ? prev?.validatedAt : undefined,
      355:         status,
      356:       };
      357:     } else {
      358:       log.push({
      359:         targetKey,
      360:         target: { ymd, hour },
      361:         picks: [],
      362:         result: null,
      363:         hit: null,
      364:         createdAt: Date.now(),
      365:         status: "empty",
      366:       });
      367:     }
      368:   }
      369: 
>>>   370:   saveTop3Log(log);
      371: }
      372: 
      373: export {
>>>   374:   getTop3Log,
>>>   375:   saveTop3Log,
      376:   registerPrediction,
      377:   registerResult,
>>>   378:   reconcilePendingTop3Log,
      379:   ensureDayTimeline,
      380:   isFutureTarget,
      381:   makeKey,
      382: };

       99: function hourBucketToSortValue(hour) {
      100:   const s = String(hour || "").trim().toLowerCase();

      154: function build20ByDezena({ grupo, baseMilhares, perCol = 5 }) {
      155:   const g = Number(grupo);
      156:   const dezenas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(g);
      157:

      165:     .filter((x) => /^\d{4}$/.test(x));
      166:

      169:

      172:     if (byDz.has(dz)) byDz.get(dz).push(m4);

      193:     seenMilhar.add(mm);

      195:   };

      206:     while (cols[dz].length < perCol) cols[dz].push("");

      208:   }
      209: 
      210:   const rows = [];
      211:   for (let r = 0; r < perCol; r += 1) {
>>>   212:     rows.push(dezenas.map((dz) => cols[dz][r] || ""));
      213:   }
      214:

      216:   return { dezenas, rows, flat20 };
      217: }

>>>   268: function analyzeTop3Hit(top3, resultGrupo, resultMilhar) {
>>>   269:   if (!Array.isArray(top3) || !top3.length) {
      270:     return { type: "none", score: 0, position: -1, matchedValue: "" };
      271:   }
      272: 
      273:   const grupoNum = Number(resultGrupo);
      274:   const milhar = normalizeMilharStr(resultMilhar);
      275:   const centena = milhar ? milhar.slice(-3) : "";
      276: 
      277:   const hasGrupo = Number.isFinite(grupoNum) && grupoNum >= 1 && grupoNum <= 25;
      278:   const hasMilhar = /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar);
      279: 
      280:   if (!hasGrupo && !hasMilhar) {
      281:     return { type: "none", score: 0, position: -1, matchedValue: "" };
      282:   }
      283: 
      284:   let best = { type: "miss", score: 0, position: -1, matchedValue: "" };
      285: 
>>>   286:   top3.forEach((item, idx) => {
      287:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
      288: 
      289:     const milharesRaw = Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)
      290:       ? item.milhares20
      291:       : Array.isArray(item?.milhares)
      292:         ? item.milhares
      293:         : [];
      294: 
      295:     const milhares = milharesRaw
>>>   296:       .map((m) => normalizeMilharStr(m))
      297:       .filter((m) => /^\d{4}$/.test(m));
      298: 
      299:     const centenas = milhares
>>>   300:       .map((m) => centenaFromMilhar(m))
      301:       .filter((c) => /^\d{3}$/.test(c));
      302: 
      303:     let score = 0;
      304:     let type = "miss";
      305:     let matchedValue = "";
      306: 
      307:     if (hasMilhar && milhares.includes(milhar)) {
      308:       score = 100;
      309:       type = "hit_exact";
      310:       matchedValue = milhar;
      311:     } else if (centena && centenas.includes(centena)) {
      312:       score = 66.67;
      313:       type = "hit_centena";
      314:       matchedValue = centena;
      315:     } else if (hasGrupo && g === grupoNum) {
      316:       score = 33.33;
      317:       type = "hit_grupo";
      318:       matchedValue = hasMilhar ? milhar.slice(-2) : formatGrupo(grupoNum);
      319:     }
      320: 
      321:     if (score > best.score) {
      322:       best = {
      323:         type,
      324:         score,
      325:         position: idx + 1,
      326:         matchedValue,
      327:       };
      328:     }
      329:   });
      330: 
      331:   return best;
      332: }

      334: function ImgWithFallback({ srcs, alt, size = 84, style }) {
      335:   const list = useMemo(
      336:     () => (Array.isArray(srcs) ? srcs.filter(Boolean) : []),
      337:     [srcs]
      338:   );
      339: 
>>>   340:   const [i, setI] = useState(0);
      341:   const listKey = useMemo(() => list.join("|"), [list]);
      342: 
      343:   useEffect(() => {
      344:     setI(0);
      345:   }, [listKey]);
      346: 
      347:   const src = list[i] || "";
      348: 
      349:   const onError = () => {
      350:     setI((prev) => (prev < list.length - 1 ? prev + 1 : prev));
      351:   };
      352: 
      353:   return (
      354:     <div
      355:       style={{
      356:         width: size,
      357:         height: size,
      358:         borderRadius: 16,
      359:         border: "1px solid rgba(201,168,62,0.36)",
      360:         background:
      361:           "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.04), rgba(0,0,0,0.28))",
      362:         display: "grid",
      363:         placeItems: "center",
      364:         overflow: "hidden",
      365:         boxShadow: "inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",
      366:         ...style,
      367:       }}
      368:     >
      369:       {src ? (
      370:         <img
      371:           src={src}
      372:           alt={alt || ""}
      373:           width={size}
      374:           height={size}
      375:           onError={onError}
      376:           style={{
      377:             width: "100%",
      378:             height: "100%",
      379:             objectFit: "cover",
      380:             display: "block",
      381:             imageRendering: "auto",
      382:           }}
      383:         />
      384:       ) : (
      385:         <div style={{ color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)", fontSize: 12 }}>
      386:           sem imagem
      387:         </div>
      388:       )}
      389:     </div>
      390:   );
      391: }

     1538:     ).length;
     1539: 
     1540:     const totalScore = validated.reduce(
     1541:       (sum, item) => sum + Number(item?.hitScore || 0),
     1542:       0
     1543:     );
     1544: 
     1545:     const average =
     1546:       validated.length > 0
     1547:         ? totalScore / validated.length
     1548:         : 0;
     1549: 
     1550:     return {
     1551:       total: historyRows.length,
     1552:       validated: validated.length,
     1553:       pending: Math.max(0, historyRows.length - validated.length),
     1554:       exact,
     1555:       centena,
     1556:       grupo,
     1557:       misses,
     1558:       average,
     1559:     };
     1560:   }, [historyRows]);
     1561: 
     1562:   const todayForCalendar = todayYMDLocalView();
     1563:   const selectedHistoryYmd = isYMD(ymdSafe)
     1564:     ? ymdSafe
     1565:     : todayForCalendar;
     1566: 
     1567:   const canGoNext =
     1568:     isYMD(selectedHistoryYmd) &&
     1569:     selectedHistoryYmd < todayForCalendar;
     1570: 
     1571:   const goToHistoryDate = useCallback(
     1572:     (nextYmd) => {
     1573:       if (!isYMD(nextYmd)) return;
     1574:       if (typeof setYmd !== "function") return;
     1575:       setYmd(nextYmd);
     1576:     },
     1577:     [setYmd]
     1578:   );
     1579: 
     1580:   return (
     1581:     <div
     1582:       style={{
     1583:         padding: 16,

     1586:         minHeight: "100%",

     1593:       }}
     1594:     >
     1595:       <style>{`

       22: function toPercent(score) {
       23:   const n = Number(score);
       24:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
       25:   const pct = n <= 1 ? n * 100 : n;
       26:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct));
       27: }
       28: 
       29: function formatGrupo(grupo) {
       30:   const g = Number(grupo);
       31:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return "—";
       32:   return String(Math.trunc(g)).padStart(2, "0");
       33: }
       34: 
       35: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
       36:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
       37:   if (!s) return "";
       38:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
       39:   if (!dig) return "";
       40:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
       41: }
       42:

       47:

       51: }

      128: function build20ByDezena({ grupo, baseMilhares, perCol = 5 }) {
      129:   const g = Number(grupo);
      130:   const dezenas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(g);
      131: 
      132:   if (!dezenas.length) {
      133:     return { dezenas: [], rows: [], flat20: [] };
      134:   }
      135: 
      136:   const input = Array.isArray(baseMilhares) ? baseMilhares : [];

>>>   138:     .map(normalizeMilharStr)
      139:     .filter((x) => /^\d{4}$/.test(x));
      140: 
      141:   const byDz = new Map();

      143: 
      144:   for (const m4 of normalized) {
      145:     const dz = dezenaFromMilhar(m4);
      146:     if (byDz.has(dz)) byDz.get(dz).push(m4);
      147:   }
      148: 
      149:   const seenCent = new Set();
      150:   const seenMilhar = new Set();
      151: 
      152:   const cols = {};

      154: 
      155:   const tryPush = (dz, m4) => {
      156:     const mm = normalizeMilharStr(m4);
      157:     if (!mm) return false;
      158: 
      159:     const c3 = centenaFromMilhar(mm);
      160:     if (!c3) return false;
      161:

      166:     seenCent.add(c3);
      167:     seenMilhar.add(mm);
      168:     return true;
      169:   };
      170: 
      171:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
      172:     const arr = byDz.get(dz) || [];

      174:       if (cols[dz].length >= perCol) break;
      175:       tryPush(dz, m4);
      176:     }
      177:   }

      179:   for (const dz of dezenas) {
      180:     while (cols[dz].length < perCol) cols[dz].push("");

      182:   }

>>>   242: function analyzeTop3Hit(top3, resultGrupo, resultMilhar) {
>>>   243:   if (!Array.isArray(top3) || !top3.length) {
      244:     return { type: "none", score: 0, position: -1 };
      245:   }
      246: 
      247:   const grupoNum = Number(resultGrupo);

      252:   const hasMilhar = /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar);
      253: 
      254:   if (!hasGrupo && !hasMilhar) {
      255:     return { type: "none", score: 0, position: -1 };
      256:   }

      259:

      262: 
      263:     const milhares = Array.isArray(item?.milhares20)

      265:       : Array.isArray(item?.milhares)
      266:         ? item.milhares
      267:         : [];

>>>   269:     const centenas = milhares.map((m) => centenaFromMilhar(m));
      270:

      272:     let type = "miss";
      273: 
      274:     if (hasMilhar && milhares.includes(milhar)) {
      275:       score = 100;
      276:       type = "hit_exact";
      277:     } else if (centena && centenas.includes(centena)) {

      279:       type = "hit_centena";
      280:     } else if (hasGrupo && g === grupoNum) {
      281:       score = 33;
      282:       type = "hit_grupo";
      283:     }

      286:       best = {
      287:         type,
      288:         score,
      289:         position: idx + 1,
      290:       };
      291:     }
      292:   });
      293: 
      294:   return best;
      295: }

      297: function ImgWithFallback({ srcs, alt, size = 84, style }) {

      306:   useEffect(() => {

      488:   const periodTo = String(item?.meta?.period?.to || "").trim();

      499:   const targetHour = String(item?.meta?.next?.hour || "").trim();
      500:

      502: 
      503:   if (periodLabel) {

      505:   }
      506: 
      507:   if (transitionDetail) {
      508:     const first = Number(transitionDetail.firstCount || 0);
>>>   509:     const top5 = Number(transitionDetail.top3Count || 0);

      559:     engineConfidence >= 75

      563:         : "FORÇA MODERADA";

>>>   758: function CompactTop3Result({ slot, status, statusLabel, statusDetail }) {
>>>   759:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
      760:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
      761:   const hasResult = Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) && resultGrupo >= 1 && resultGrupo <= 25;
      762:   const resultAnimal = hasResult ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "") : "";
      763:   const resultImg = hasResult ? getImgFromGrupo(resultGrupo, 64) : "";
      764: 
      765:   return (
      766:     <div className="timeline-compact">

      768:         <div className="timeline-compact__predictions">
>>>   769:           {slotTop3.map((item, idx) => {
      770:             const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
      771:             const animal = String(item?.animal || getAnimalLabel(grupo) || "").trim();
      772:             const img = Number.isFinite(grupo) ? getImgFromGrupo(grupo, 64) : "";
      773: 
      774:             return (
      775:               <div
      776:                 key={`${String(slot?.targetYmd || "y")}_${String(slot?.targetHour || "h")}_${idx}_${grupo}`}

      779:                 <div className="timeline-compact__pickRank">{idx + 1}</div>
      780:                 <ImgWithFallback
      781:                   srcs={img ? [img] : []}
      782:                   alt={animal || `G${formatGrupo(grupo)}`}
      783:                   size={44}
      784:                   style={{ borderRadius: 10 }}
      785:                 />
      786:                 <div className="timeline-compact__pickText">
      787:                   <div className="timeline-compact__pickGroup">G{formatGrupo(grupo)}</div>
      788:                   <div className="timeline-compact__pickAnimal">
      789:                     {animal ? animal.toUpperCase() : "—"}
      790:                   </div>
      791:                 </div>
      792:               </div>
      793:             );
      794:           })}
      795:         </div>
      796: 
      797:         <div className="timeline-compact__result">

      799: 
      800:           {hasResult ? (
      801:             <div className="timeline-compact__resultBox">
      802:               <ImgWithFallback
      803:                 srcs={resultImg ? [resultImg] : []}
      804:                 alt={resultAnimal}
      805:                 size={48}
      806:                 style={{ borderRadius: 10 }}
      807:               />
      808:               <div className="timeline-compact__resultText">

      810:                 <div className="timeline-compact__resultAnimal">

      812:                 </div>

      823:           >
      824:             {statusLabel}
      825:           </div>
      826:           <div className="timeline-compact__performanceText">{statusDetail}</div>
      827:         </div>
      828:       </div>
      829:     </div>
      830:   );

      833: function TimelineSlot({
      834:   slot,
      835:   theme,
      836:   copiedAllKey,
      837:   copiedCellKey,
      838:   setCopiedAllKey,
      839:   setCopiedCellKey,
      840:   copyText,
      841:   build16,
      842:   buildMilhares,
      843:   build20,
      844: }) {
      845:   const t = theme;
>>>   846:   const slotTop3 = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
      847: 
      848:   const resultGrupo = getSlotResultGrupo(slot);
      849:   const hasResult = hasOfficialResult(slot);
      850:   const resultAnimal =
      851:     hasResult && Number.isFinite(resultGrupo)
      852:       ? String(getAnimalLabel(resultGrupo) || "")
      853:       : "";
      854: 
>>>   855:   const analysis = analyzeTop3Hit(
>>>   856:     slotTop3,
      857:     resultGrupo,
      858:     extractResultMilhar(slot)
      859:   );
      860: 
      861:   const status =
      862:     !hasResult
      863:       ? "pending"
      864:       : analysis.type === "miss"
      865:         ? "miss"
      866:         : "hit";
      867: 
      868:   const statusLabel =
      869:     !hasResult
      870:       ? "⏳ PENDENTE"
      871:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
      872:         ? `🎯 ACERTO TOTAL (${analysis.score}%)`
      873:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
      874:           ? `🟡 CENTENA (${analysis.score}%)`
      875:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
      876:             ? `✅ GRUPO (${analysis.score}%)`
      877:             : "❌ ERRO (0%)";
      878: 
      879:   const statusDetail =
      880:     !hasResult
      881:       ? "Aguardando resultado oficial."
      882:       : analysis.type === "hit_exact"
      883:         ? `Milhar exata acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
      884:         : analysis.type === "hit_centena"
      885:           ? `Centena acertada no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
      886:           : analysis.type === "hit_grupo"
      887:             ? `Grupo acertado no palpite #${analysis.position}.`
      888:             : "Nenhum dos 3 palpites foi sorteado.";
      889: 
      890:   return (
>>>   891:     <section className="top3-shell">
      892:       <div className="timeline-slot__header">
      893:         <div className="timeline-slot__title">
      894:           {formatYmdHour(slot?.targetYmd, slot?.targetHour)}
      895:         </div>
      896: 
      897:         <div className={`timeline-slot__badge timeline-slot__badge--${status}`}>
      898:           {statusLabel}
      899:         </div>
      900:       </div>
      901: 
      902:       <div className="timeline-slot__metaGrid">
>>>   903:         <div className="top3-metaItem">
>>>   904:           <div className="top3-metaItem__label">Base usada</div>
>>>   905:           <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
      906:             {formatYmdHour(slot?.baseYmd, slot?.baseHour)}
      907:           </div>
      908:         </div>
      909: 
>>>   910:         <div className="top3-metaItem">
>>>   911:           <div className="top3-metaItem__label">Resultado real</div>
>>>   912:           <div className="top3-metaItem__value">
      913:             {hasResult
      914:               ? `G${formatGrupo(resultGrupo)} • ${resultAnimal.toUpperCase()}`
      915:               : "Pendente"}
      916:           </div>
      917:         </div>
      918: 
>>>   919:         <div className="top3-metaItem">
>>>   920:           <div className="top3-metaItem__label">Status</div>
>>>   921:           <div className="top3-metaItem__value">{statusDetail}</div>
      922:         </div>
      923:       </div>
      924: 
>>>   925:       {!slotTop3.length ? (
>>>   926:         <div className="top3-empty">Sem Top3 calculado para este horário.</div>
      927:       ) : status === "pending" ? (
      928:         <div className="timeline-slot__cards">
>>>   929:           {slotTop3.map((item, i) => (
>>>   930:             <Top3Card
      931:               key={`${slot?.targetYmd || "y"}_${slot?.targetHour || "h"}_${String(item?.grupo ?? i)}`}
      932:               item={item}
      933:               idx={i}
      934:               theme={t}
      935:               copiedAllKey={copiedAllKey}
      936:               copiedCellKey={copiedCellKey}
      937:               setCopiedAllKey={setCopiedAllKey}
      938:               setCopiedCellKey={setCopiedCellKey}
      939:               copyText={copyText}
      940:               build16={build16}
      941:               buildMilhares={buildMilhares}
      942:               build20={build20}
      943:             />
      944:           ))}
      945:         </div>
      946:       ) : (
>>>   947:         <CompactTop3Result
      948:           slot={slot}
      949:           status={status}
      950:           statusLabel={statusLabel}
      951:           statusDetail={statusDetail}
      952:         />
      953:       )}
      954:     </section>
      955:   );
      956: }

>>>   958: export default function Top3View(props) {
      959:   const {
      960:     loading,
      961:     error,
>>>   962:     timelineTop3,
      963:     layerMetaText,
      964:     lastLabel,
      965:     prevLabel,
      966:     theme,
>>>   967:     LOTTERY_OPTIONS,
>>>   968:     lotteryKeySafe,
>>>   969:     setLotteryKey,

      973:     analysisYmd,
      974:     analysisHourBucket,
      975:     lastHourBucket,
      976:   } = props || {};
      977: 
      978:   const forecastSlot = useMemo(() => {
>>>   979:   if (!Array.isArray(timelineTop3) || !timelineTop3.length) return null;
      980: 
>>>   981:   return timelineTop3.find((slot) =>
      982:     String(slot?.targetYmd || "").trim() === String(analysisYmd || "").trim() &&
      983:     String(slot?.targetHour || "").trim() === String(analysisHourBucket || "").trim()
      984:   ) || null;
>>>   985: }, [timelineTop3, analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket]);
      986: 
>>>   987: const list = Array.isArray(forecastSlot?.top3)
>>>   988:   ? forecastSlot.top3.slice(0, 3)
      989:   : [];
>>>   990:   const timeline = (Array.isArray(timelineTop3) ? timelineTop3 : [])
      991:   .slice()
      992:   .sort((a, b) => {
      993:     const ta = `${a?.targetYmd || ""} ${a?.targetHour || ""}`;
      994:     const tb = `${b?.targetYmd || ""} ${b?.targetHour || ""}`;
      995:     return ta.localeCompare(tb);
      996:   });
      997: 
      998:   const showDetailedTimeline = false;
      999: 
     1000:   const meta = useMemo(() => {
     1001:     const last = lastLabel || "—";
     1002:     const prev = prevLabel || "—";
     1003:     const layer = cleanLayerText(layerMetaText || "—");
     1004:     return { last, prev, layer };
     1005:   }, [lastLabel, prevLabel, layerMetaText]);
     1006: 
     1007:   const headerBase = useMemo(() => meta.last || "—", [meta.last]);
     1008: 
     1009:   const headerForecast = useMemo(() => {
     1010:     return formatYmdHour(analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket);
     1011:   }, [analysisYmd, analysisHourBucket]);
     1012: 
     1013:   const headerTransition = useMemo(() => {
     1014:     const from = String(lastHourBucket || "").trim();
     1015:     const to = String(analysisHourBucket || "").trim();
     1016:     if (from && to) return `${from} → ${to}`;
     1017:     return "—";
     1018:   }, [lastHourBucket, analysisHourBucket]);
     1019: 
     1020:   const t = theme || {
     1021:     bg: "#050505",
     1022:     panel: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)",
